# JDK classes the Windows app loads, for the class-data-sharing archive that packaging writes into
# the bundled runtime (runtime/bin/server/classes.jsa). See generateWindowsCdsArchive in
# composeApp/build.gradle.kts and Docs/PERFORMANCE-AUDIT-2026-10.md section 9 in nuvio-z.
#
# Recorded on the shipped Temurin 17 runtime with -XX:DumpLoadedClassList over launch -> profile
# picker -> Home -> Continue Watching and catalog scrolling -> Library and back
# (scripts/perf/phase2.py scroll), then filtered to JDK classes (scripts/perf/cds_variants.py).
# Entries a runtime does not have are skipped with a warning when the archive is dumped, so a stale
# list only shrinks the archive; it never changes behaviour. Regenerate after a JDK or Compose upgrade.
